    <!-- Sh'elah's own type (tokens.css). The two Latin faces every page draws
         first are preloaded; the metric-matched fallbacks keep the swap from
         moving the page. -->

    <!-- The CSS the first paint of this kind of page uses, inline (the paint no
         longer waits for a stylesheet request), and the whole stylesheet bundle
         behind it, which applies as soon as it has loaded. The bundle holds
         every sheet listed in the fallback below, in that order, minified; the
         critical CSS is the subset of it that matches the server-rendered page
         (scripts/build_critical_css.mjs, npm run build:critical). -->
    
    
    
    

    <!-- Libraries a visit may never need are fetched when something asks for them,
         not with the page (each pinned, with its SRI hash, as the script tags
         were): FullCalendar when the calendar is about to open (shell.js
         openCalendarModal and the calendar button's hover/focus/touch), Clerk
         at once for a visit that may be signed in and otherwise when someone
         reaches for the account (shell.js ensureClerk), Motion
         once the visitor moves or a few seconds after load (static/js/motion.js
         falls back to instant state changes until then).
         window.ShelahLibs.load(name) resolves true once the library's global
         exists, false if it could not be loaded; callers keep going without it.
         An entry's deps load first, in order (Clerk 6 needs @clerk/ui's
         window.__internal_ClerkUICtor before Clerk.load; FullCalendar 7 needs
         Temporal, which the polyfill only supplies where the browser lacks it,
         and its Classic theme needs the core). An entry's css loads before its
         script, so the calendar never paints unstyled. -->
    
    
    

    <!-- Cloudflare Turnstile (Managed) -- anti-abuse gate for anonymous /ask
         traffic (backend/turnstile.py, docs/SECURITY.md §8). Token callbacks
         are defined here, ahead of the api.js script tag, so there is no
         race between Turnstile's own async/defer script finishing its
         (near-instant, invisible-mode) check and our callback existing --
         window.__turnstileToken is read later by handleAiSearch() /
         ai-service.js when they build the /ask request body. True no-op
         (no script tag at all, no live network call to Cloudflare) unless
         BOTH the feature flag is on and a site key is configured -- a site
         key can be present in an environment's secrets well before the
         flag is flipped on (a deploy-time decision, not this markup's),
         and the widget must not start silently phoning home before then. -->
    
    

    <!-- Vercel Speed Insights -->
    
    
</head>

    <!-- The app's main script (static/js/shell.js). It was an inline script here,
         ~600 KB of the HTML fetched and parsed ahead of the first paint; it is a
         deferred file now. Order matters, so the dispatcher and the Motion CDN
         script that follow are deferred too and keep their place after it: this
         script registers the capture listener that records the clicked control,
         and actions.js's has to run after it (tests/test_inline_handlers.py). -->
    
    <!-- Delegated data-onclick / data-oninput dispatcher (replaces inline
         onclick="..."; the CSP forbids those). It must come AFTER the inline
         script above: its click listener is registered on `document` in the
         capture phase, and capture listeners run in registration order. The
         script above registers the one that records the clicked control
         (markTriggerPending reads it), and an inline onclick used to run after
         every capture listener, so this one has to as well.
         tests/test_inline_handlers.py pins the order. -->
    
    <!-- Motion One / motion.dev (vanilla animation engine): loaded by window.ShelahLibs
         (head); static/js/motion.js wraps it as window.Motion and degrades gracefully
         to instant state-changes until it has loaded (see its _motionAnimate() fallback). -->
